       Medical Support Services                                                   醫療支持服務

       Our medical support services are designed to help individuals              我們的醫療支持服務旨在幫助個人管理日常
       manage their everyday health and wellness, and are further                 健康，並進一步分為(i)健康會員計劃及(ii)其

Non-IFRS Measure                                                         非國際財務報告準則計量
To supplement the Group’s consolidated financial statements,             為補充本集團根據國際財務報告準則列報的綜合財
which are presented in accordance with IFRSs, the Group also uses        務報表，本集團在2022至2024財政年度還採用經
adjusted net profit (non-IFRS measure) (as defined below) as an          調整淨利潤（非國際財務報告準則計量）
                                                                                          （定義見下
additional financial measure for the financial years 2022 to 2024,       文）作為額外財務計量，其並非國際財務報告準則
which is not required by, or presented in accordance with IFRSs.         的要求，或按照國際財務報告準則所呈列。


The Group defines “adjusted net profit (non-IFRS measure)” as            本集團將經調整淨利潤（非國際財務報告準則計量）
loss for the year, adding back changes in carrying amount of the         定義為年內虧損，並加回贖回負債賬面值變動及上
redemption liabilities and listing expenses. The carrying amount         市開支。贖回負債的賬面值屬非現金性質。上市開
of the redemption liabilities are non-cash in nature. The listing        支為與全球發售有關的開支。
expenses are expenses related to global offering.


The adjusted net profit (non-IFRS measure) can provide useful            經調整淨利潤（非國際財務報告準則計量）通過與其
information to investors in understanding and evaluating the             幫助本集團管理層的相同方式，為投資者在了解及
Group’s consolidated results of operations in the same manner as         評估本集團的綜合經營業績時提供有用資料。
it helps the Group’s management.


As the preferential rights were terminated upon the listing of the       由於優先權利已於本集團的股份在聯交所主板上市
Group’s shares on the Main Board of the Stock Exchange and the           時終止，相關贖回負債已重新分類至權益，所以
related redemption liabilities were reclassified to equity, adjusted     2025年度已無需採用經調整淨利潤作為額外財務
net profit was no longer necessary as an additional financial            計量。
measure for the year 2025.


For comparison purposes, adjusted net profit for the financial year      作為對比，2024財政年度經調整淨利潤為22.8百
2024 was RMB22.8 million, and net profit for the financial year          萬元，2025財政年度淨利潤為54.3百萬元。
2025 was RMB54.3 million.

     Dividend Policy                                                       股息政策
     The Company has adopted a dividend policy that aims to balance        本公司已採納股息政策，旨在平衡兩大目標：(i)透
     two objectives: (i) providing shareholders with a fair return         過參與本公司利潤為股東提供合理回報；及(ii)保留
     through participation in the Company’s profits; and (ii) retaining    足夠儲備以支持本集團的長遠增長及財務穩定。
     sufficient reserves to support the Group’s long-term growth and
     financial stability.

RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL                                              風險管理及內部控制
CONTROLS
The Board acknowledges its responsibility for the risk management         董事會承認其須對風險管理及內部監控系統負責，
and internal control systems and reviewing their effectiveness.           並有責任檢討該等制度的有效性。該等系統旨在管
Such systems are designed to manage rather than eliminate                 理而非消除未能達成業務目標的風險，而且只能就
the risk of failure to achieve business objectives, and can only          不會有重大的失實陳述或損失作出合理而非絕對的
provide reasonable and not absolute assurance against material            保證。
misstatement or loss.


The Board has the overall responsibility for evaluating and               董事會整體負責評估及釐定本公司達成策略目標時
determining the nature and extent of the risks it is willing to take      所願意接納的風險性質及程度，並確保設立及維持
in achieving the Company’s strategic objectives, and establishing         合適及有效的風險管理及內部監控系統。
and maintaining appropriate and effective risk management and
internal control systems.


The Audit Committee assists the Board in leading the management           審核委員會協助董事會領導管理層及監督彼等對風
and overseeing their design, implementation and monitoring of             險管理及內部控制系統的設計、實施、監察及有效
the risk management and internal control systems.                         性。


The Company’s risk management and internal control systems                本公司已制定其風險管理及內部控制系統，其具備
have been developed with the following features and processes:            以下特點及流程


(a)   The processes used to identify, evaluate and manage                 (a)   本集團用於識別、評估及管理重大風險的程
      significant risks by the Group are summarised as follows:                 序概述如下：


      Risk Identification: Identifies risks that may potentially affect         風險識別：識別可能對本集團的業務及營運
      the Group’s business and operations.                                      造成潛在影響之風險。


      Risk Assessment: Assesses the risks identified by using the               風險評估：利用管理層制定之評估標準評估
      assessment criteria developed by the management; and                      所識別的風險；及考慮對業務之影響及後果
      considers the impact and consequence on the business and                  以及發生之可能性。
      the likelihood of their occurrence.


      Risk Response: Prioritises the risks by comparing the results             風險應對：透過比較風險評估的結果確定風
      of the risk assessment; and determines the risk management                險的優先順序；及釐定風險管理策略及內部
      strategies and internal control processes to prevent, avoid or            控制程序以預防、避免或減輕該等風險。
      mitigate the risks.

Convening an Extraordinary General Meeting                              召開股東特別大會
Pursuant to Article 9.3 of the Company’s Articles of Association,       根據本公司組織章程細則第9.3條，董事會可酌情
The Board may, whenever it thinks fit, convene an extraordinary         召開股東特別大會。股東特別大會亦須應一名或多
general meeting. Extraordinary general meetings shall also be           名於遞呈要求日期持有不少於本公司有權於股東大
convened on the requisition of one or more Shareholders holding,        會上投票的實繳股本十分之一的股東要求而予以召
at the date of deposit of the requisition, not less than one tenth of   開。該項要求須以書面向董事會或秘書提呈，以要
the paid up capital of the Company having the right of voting at        求董事會召開股東特別大會處理該要求中所列明的
general meetings. Such requisition shall be made in writing to the      任何事項。有關大會須於遞交有關要求後2個月內
Board or a Secretary for the purpose of requiring an extraordinary      舉行。如董事會於遞交要求之日起計21日內未有
general meeting to be called by the Board for the transaction           召開該會議，則遞交要求人士可按相同方式自行召
of any business specified in such requisition. Such meeting shall       開會議，而本公司須向要求人士償付因董事會的不
be held within two months after the deposit of such requisition.        作為而令要求人士招致的所有合理開支。
If within 21 days of such deposit, the Board fails to proceed to
convene such meeting, the requisitionist(s) himself (themselves)
may do so in the same manner, and all reasonable expenses
incurred by the requisitionist(s) as a result of the failure of the
Board shall be reimbursed to the requisitionist(s) by the Company.

       Information security and privacy protection                        信息安全與隱私保護
       HealthyWay strictly complies with laws and regulations             健康之路嚴格遵守《中華人民共和國網絡安全
       such as the Cybersecurity Law of the People’s Republic             法》、《中華人民共和國個人信息保護法》等法
       of China and the Personal Information Protection Law               律法規，致力於保護數據私隱和安全，我們
       of the People’s Republic of China , and is committed to            已就平台整體制定並實施嚴格的數據收集、
       protecting data privacy and security. We have formulated           處理和使用政策。2025年度，我們通過了
       and implemented stringent policies on data collection,             ISO 27001信息安全管理體系認證，重點關注
       processing and use for our platform as a whole. In 2025, we        數據安全和個人信息保護。集團設立應急小
       obtained the ISO 27001 Information Security Management             組，定期開展重要系統災難恢復演練並持續
       System certification, with a focus on data security and            優化系統性能。後端安全防禦體系可有效抵
       personal information protection. The Group has established         禦網絡惡意攻擊，切實保障平台運行安全與
       an emergency response team, conducts regular disaster              用戶隱私。
       recovery drills for critical systems, and continuously optimizes
       system performance. The backend security defence
       system can effectively resist malicious attacks, effectively
       safeguarding the secure operation of the platform and user
       privacy.


       To ensure data confidentiality and integrity, the Group            為保障數據保密性與完整性，集團實施嚴格
       has implemented a rigorous data security management                的數據安全管理方案，通過多節點、獨立離
       plan. By utilizing a multi-node, independent offline backup        線備份機制將數據丟失風險降至最低，並定
       mechanism, we minimize the risk of data loss and regularly         期檢查備份系統運行狀態，確保穩定可靠。

various customer incidents. The Group has also issued the        得到及時、高效解決。集團將投訴劃分為普

which further refines the complaint handling procedures,         訴需在24小時內制定處理方案並回覆客戶，
processing principles and work responsibilities, ensuring that   重大投訴須立即通過OA及電話上報，並在4
customer demands are addressed promptly and efficiently.         小時內完成方案制定與反饋；危機投訴將第
The Group classifies complaints into three categories:           一時間報送最高分管領導，由其統籌協調資
ordinary complaints, major complaints and crisis complaints.     源，快速制定危機處理方案。
For ordinary complaints, a handling plan must be formulated
and a response provided to the customer within 24 hours.
Major complaints must be reported immediately via OA
and telephone, with a plan formulated and feedback
provided within 4 hours. Crisis complaints will be reported
to the highest ranking supervising leader at the earliest
opportunity, who will coordinate resources and swiftly
formulate a crisis handling plan.

        Case: “Tear Off the Labels, Refuse to Be Defined” Campaign
        案例：「撕掉標籤，拒絕被定義」的主題活動

        To mark International Women’s Day in March 2025, HealthyWay organized a special campaign themed
        “Tear Off the Labels, Refuse to Be Defined”. A wall of stereotypes was set up at the event, and women
        enthusiastically participated by tearing down the labels and making declarations, expressing their resistance
        to gender bias and their determination to define themselves. In addition, the event featured a raffle with
        a variety of thoughtful prizes, demonstrating care and consideration for women. This campaign aims to
        encourage women to break free from constraints, be true to themselves, and live a fulfilling life that defies
        conventional definitions.
        在2025年3月的三八國際婦女節，健康之路特別策劃了一場「撕掉標籤，拒絕被定義」的主題活動。活動
        現場設置了一面寫滿刻板印象的標籤牆，女性朋友們踴躍參與，撕掉標籤並發表宣言，表達了對性別偏
        見的反抗和自我定義的決心。此外，活動還設有抽獎環節，提供各種貼心獎品，表達了對女性的關愛與
        呵護。這場活動旨在鼓勵女性打破束縛，勇敢做自己，活出不被定義的精彩人生。

        Case: Hosting a Themed Sharing Session

        2025年3月，福建省數字經濟促進會數字健康專委會在健康之路總部成功舉辦主題分享會，近30位專
        家學者和企業代表參加。健康之路展示了其在數字醫療領域的成就，並分享了在AI大模型方面的創新實

        perspectives of national policies, industry trends, stakeholder   並研究相關應對策略，以減少氣候變化對集
        concerns, etc. and studies relevant response strategies to        團業務、財務的不利影響。本集團進行了
        reduce the adverse impact of climate change on the Group’s        ESG相關的風險評估，其中包括識別本集團
        business and finances. The Group has conducted an ESG-            潛在的氣候相關風險包括會產生洪水、極端
        related risk assessment, which includes identifying the           天氣或氣溫上升等物理風險，亦可能帶來由
        Group’s potential climate-related risks such as flooding,         市場、監管、政策等變化而引起的轉型風險
        extreme weather, rising temperatures or other physical risks,     等。我們定期評估現有應對措施的有效性，
        as well as transition risks caused by changes in the market,      加入了氣候風險可能對運營中價值鏈的影

        the effectiveness of our existing measures and factor in the      務特性主要以線上運營平台為主，我們認為
        potential impact of climate risks on our operational value        氣候變化對公司經營及財務不會帶來負面影
        chain to evaluate the Company’s climate resilience. Given         響。未來，我們會持續關注氣候變化對財務
        that the Company’s business primarily consists of online          的影響，並預計氣候變化對於財務業績及現

        information to ensure that early warning information is         具體詳見「企業管治與風險管控」章節內容。

The Group is committed to conducting its business in an                 本集團致力以環保的方式開展業務，並儘量減少其

effects caused by its operations on the environment. The Group          所採取各種綠色措施，致力節約能源及減少不必要

light tubes, encouraging use of recycle papers and both sides of        本集團業務營運中實施進一步環保措施及常規。
papers for printing and copying and keeping office temperature

of the Group’s business.

     Reasons for using the Contractual Arrangements                         使用合約安排的原因

     Due to foreign ownership restrictions under the relevant               基於相關中國法律、法規及監管措施下的外
     PRC laws, regulations and regulatory practices, it is not              商投資限制，(i)通過股權直接持有福建健康
     viable for our Company to (i) hold Fujian Health Road and              之路及其附屬公司進行我們的遠程醫療諮詢
     its subsidiaries directly through equity ownership to conduct          服務，或(ii)直接持有福建健康醫療科技多於
     our remote medical consultation services, or (ii) directly hold        50%股權進行增值電信服務業務，對本公司
     more than 50% equity interests in Fujian Health MedTech                而言均為不可行。相反，本公司通過合約安
     to conduct our value-added telecommunications services                 排控制該等實體的全部或部分股權，並得以
     businesses. Rather, the Company control all or part of the             合併併表聯屬實體的所有經濟利益。有關詳
     equity interests in these entities through the Contractual             情，請參閱招股章程「合約安排」。
     Arrangements, through which we are able to consolidate
     all the economic benefits of the Consolidated Affiliated
     Entities. For details, see “Contractual Arrangements” in the
     prospectus.

Loss Sharing                                                                虧損分擔

Neither the agreements under the Contractual Arrangements nor               合約安排項下的協議或中國法律均無規定或要求本
the PRC laws provide or require that our Company or Health Road             公司或健康之路健康科技有義務分擔併表聯屬實體
HealthTech be obligated to share the losses of the Consolidated             的虧損或向併表聯屬實體提供財務資助。此外，各
Affiliated Entities or provide financial assistance to the                  併表聯屬實體為獨立法律實體，須自行承擔其自身
Consolidated Affiliated Entities. Further, each of the Consolidated         債務及虧損。
Affiliated Entities is a separate legal entity and shall be solely liable
for its own debts and losses.


Despite the foregoing, our business, financial condition and results        儘管上文所述，倘併表聯屬實體蒙受虧損，我們的
of operations would be adversely affected if the Consolidated               業務、財務狀況及經營業績將受到不利影響，原因
Affiliated Entities suffer losses given that (i) our Group conducts         為(i)本集團通過持有必要中國許可證及批文的併表
businesses in the PRC through the Consolidated Affiliated Entities          聯屬實體在中國開展業務，及(ii)併表聯屬實體的財
which hold the requisite PRC licenses and approvals, and (ii) the           務狀況及經營業績根據適用會計原則併入本公司的
Consolidated Affiliated Entities’ financial condition and results           財務報表，因此，合約安排的條文乃為此而設，以
of operations are consolidated into our Company’s financial                 儘可能限制併表聯屬實體蒙受任何虧損對健康之路
statements under the applicable accounting principles, therefore,           健康科技及本公司的潛在不利影響。
the provisions in the Contractual Arrangements are tailored so
as to limit, to the greatest extent possible, the potential adverse
effect on Health Road HealthTech and our Company that may be
suffered by any loss from the Consolidated Affiliated Entities.

OTHER CONNECTED TRANSACTIONS                                         其它關連交易
During the Year, Zhejiang Health Road Technology Group Co.,          本年度內，本公司全資附屬子公司浙江健康之路科
Ltd., a wholly-owned subsidiary of the Company, entered into an      技集團有限公司於2025年12月24日訂立一份股權
equity transfer agreement dated 24 December 2025 to dispose          轉讓協議，以出售本公司間接非全資附屬公司健宸
of 51% of shares interest of Jianchen Medical, an indirect non-      醫藥51%的股權，總代價為人民幣1,677,900元。
wholly-owned subsidiary of the Company, at an aggregate              買方為健宸醫藥的主要股東及董事，根據上市規則
consideration of RMB1,677,900. The purchaser is a substantial        第14A.07(1)條，買方為本公司附屬公司層面的關
shareholder as well as a director of Jianchen Medical; therefore,    連人士，此出售事項構成本公司的關連交易。根據
the purchaser is a connected person of the Company at the            上市規則第14A.101條，由於(1)買方為附屬公司層
subsidiary level according to Rule 14A.07(1) of the Listing Rules.   面的關連人士；(2)董事會已批准出售事項；及(3)
Accordingly, the disposal constituted a connected transaction of     獨立非執行董事已確認出售事項的條款屬公平合理
the Company under Chapter 14A of the Listing Rules. By virtue        及出售事項按一般商業條款或更佳條款訂立且符合
of Rule 14A.101 of the Listing Rules, since (1) the purchaser is     本公司及其股東的整體利益，故出售事項須遵守上
a connected person at the subsidiary level, (2) the Board has        市規則第14A章項下的申報及公告規定，惟獲豁免
approved the disposal; and (3) the independent non-executive         遵守通函、獨立財務意見及獨立股東批准規定。概
Directors have confirmed that the terms of the disposal are          無董事被視為於出售事項中擁有重大權益，故概無
fair and reasonable and the disposal is on normal commercial         董事須就批准股權轉讓協議及其項下擬進行交易的
terms or better and in the interests of the Company and its          董事會決議案放棄投票。
Shareholders as a whole, the disposal is subject to the reporting
and announcement requirements but is exempt from the circular,
independent financial advice and independent Shareholders,
approval requirements under Chapter 14A of the Listing Rules.
None of the Directors is considered to have a material interest in
the Disposal and therefore, none of the Directors was required
to abstain from voting on the Board resolutions approving the
Equity Transfer Agreement and the transactions contemplated
thereunder.

      OPINION                                                               意見
      We have audited the consolidated financial statements of              我們已審核第175至275頁所載健康之路股份有限
      HealthyWay Inc. (“the Company”) and its subsidiaries (“the            公司（「貴公司」）及其附屬公司（「貴集團」）的綜合財
      Group”) set out on pages 175 to 275, which comprise the               務報表，此財務報表包括於2025年12月31日的綜
      consolidated statement of financial position as at 31 December        合財務狀況表與截至該日止年度的綜合損益表、綜
      2025, the consolidated statement of profit or loss, the               合損益及其他全面收益表，綜合權益變動表及綜合

2   MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES (Continued)                       2   主要會計政策（續）
    (b) Basis of preparation of the financial                          (b) 財務報表之編製基準
        statements
        The consolidated financial statements for the year                 截至2025年12月31日止年度的綜合財
        ended 31 December 2025 comprise the Company                        務報表包括本公司、本公司控制的實體
        and the entities controlled by the Company and the                 以及本集團於聯營公司之權益。
        Group’s interest in associates.


        The measurement basis used in the preparation of the               編製財務報表所使用的計量基準為歷史
        financial statements is the historical cost basis except           成本基準，其他金融資產除外（見附註
        for other financial assets (see Note 2(g)).                        2(g)）。


        The preparation of financial statements in conformity              編製符合國際財務報告準則會計準則的
        with IFRS Accounting Standards requires management                 財務報表須管理層作出判斷、估計及假
        to make judgements, estimates and assumptions                      設，從而影響政策應用及資產及負債、
        that affect the application of policies and reported               收入及開支的呈報金額。估計及相關假
        amounts of assets and liabilities, income and                      設基於過往經驗及在具體情況下認為合
        expenses. The estimates and associated assumptions                 理的各項其他因素作出，所得結果用作
        are based on historical experience and various other               判斷目前顯然無法從其他來源確定的資
        factors that are believed to be reasonable under the               產及負債的賬面值的依據。實際結果或
        circumstances, the results of which form the basis of              有別於該等估計。
        making the judgements about carrying values of assets
        and liabilities that are not readily apparent from other
        sources. Actual results may differ from these estimates.


        The estimates and the underlying assumptions are                   估計及相關假設予以持續檢討。倘修訂
        reviewed on an ongoing basis. Revisions to accounting              會計估計僅影響修訂估計期間，則有關
        estimates are recognised in the period in which the                修訂於該期間確認，或倘修訂影響現時
        estimate is revised if the revision affects only that              及未來期間，則於修訂期間及未來期間
        period, or in the period of the revision and future                確認。
        periods if the revision affects both current and future
        periods.


        Judgements made by management in the application                   有關管理層在應用國際財務報告準則會
        of IFRS Accounting Standards that have significant                 計準則時所作出對財務報表有重大影響
        effect on the financial statements and major sources               的判斷，以及估計不確定因素主要來源
        of estimation uncertainty are discussed in Note 3.                 的討論載於附註3。

                 on which control commences until the date on which                   止。
                 control ceases.

    (j)   Leased assets                                                     (j)   租賃資產
          At inception of a contract, the Group assesses whether                  於合約開始時，本集團評估合約是否為
          the contract is, or contains, a lease. This is the case if              租賃或包含租賃。倘合約賦予權利於一
          the contract conveys the right to control the use of                    段時間內控制已識別資產的用途以換取
          an identified asset for a period of time in exchange                    代價，則該合約為租賃或包含租賃。倘
          for consideration. Control is conveyed where the                        客戶既有權指示已識別資產的使用，亦
          customer has both the right to direct the use of the                    有權自該使用中獲得絕大部分經濟利
          identified asset and to obtain substantially all of the                 益，則控制權已轉移。
          economic benefits from that use.

                and accounts for each lease component and any                           分及任何相關非租賃部分作為單
                associated non-lease components as a single                             一租賃部分列賬。

                       When the Group enters into a lease in respect                          租賃除外。當本集團就低價值資

                       term, discounted using the interest rate implicit                      或倘該利率不能輕易釐定，則以
                       in the lease or, if that rate cannot be readily                        相關遞增借款利率貼現。於初步

                       borrowing rate. After initial recognition, the                         量，而利息開支則採用實際利率

                       interest method. Variable lease payments that do                       付款，因此可變租賃付款於其產
                       not depend on an index or rate are not included                        生的會計期間在損益中扣除。
                       in the measurement of the lease liability, and

                restore the underlying asset or the site on which                     成本，並減去任何收到的租賃減
                it is located, discounted to their present value,                     免。使用權資產隨後按成本減累

                whether it will exercise a purchase, extension                        則租賃負債將重新計量。倘以這

                The Group recognises a loss allowance for                             本集團就以下項目的預期信貸虧
                expected credit losses (“ECL”s) on:                                   損（「預期信貸虧損」）確認虧損撥
                                                                                      備：


                –     financial assets measured at amortised                          －    按攤銷成本計量的金融資

                Measurement of ECLs                                                   預期信貸虧損的計量

                ECLs are a probability-weighted estimate                              預期信貸虧損為信貸虧損的概率
                of credit losses. Generally, credit losses are                        加權估計。一般而言，信貸虧損
                measured as the present value of all expected                         以合約及預期金額的所有預期現
                cash shortfalls between the contractual and                           金差額的現值計量。
                expected amounts.


                For undrawn loan commitments, expected                                就未提取的貸款承諾而言，預期
                cash shortfalls are measured as the difference                        現金差額乃按以下兩者之間的差
                between (i) the contractual cash flows that                           額計算：(i)倘貸款承諾持有人提
                would be due to the Group if the holder of the                        取該筆貸款，本集團應收的合約
                loan commitment draws down on the loan and                            現金流量；及(ii)倘該筆貸款被提
                (ii) the cash flows that the Group expects to                         取，本集團預期將收到的現金流
                receive if the loan is drawn down.                                    量。


                The expected cash shortfalls are discounted                           倘影響重大，則預期現金差額將
                using the following discount rates if the effect is                   採用以下貼現率貼現：
                material:


                –     fixed-rate financial assets, trade and                          －    固定利率的金融資產、貿
                      other receivables and contract assets:                               易及其他應收款項以及合
                      effective interest rate determined at initial                        約資產： 於初始確認時釐
                      recognition or an approximation thereof;                             定的實際利率或其近似
                      and                                                                  值；及


                –     variable-rate financial assets: current                         －    浮息金融資產： 即期實際
                      effective interest rate.                                             利率。

                        For impairment testing, assets are grouped                                就減值測試而言，資產將歸入最

                        are largely independent of the cash inflows of                            資產或現金產生單位（「現金產生

                        Goodwill arising from a business combination is                           生的商譽分配至預期可從合併協

                        the greater of its value in use and its fair value                        額為其使用價值與公允價值減
                        less costs of disposal. Value in use is based on                          處置成本兩者中的較高者。使用
                        the estimated future cash flows, discounted to                            價值基於估計未來現金流量，並
                        their present value using a pre-tax discount rate                         使用反映當前市場對貨幣時間價
                        that reflects current market assessments of the                           值及資產或現金產生單位特定風
                        time value of money and the risks specific to the                         險評估的稅前折現率折現至其現
                        asset or CGU.                                                             值。

                        recoverable amount.                                                       虧損。


                        Impairment losses are recognised in profit                                減值虧損於損益中確認。減值虧
                        or loss. They are allocated first to reduce the                           損首先分配以減少分配至現金產

                             when the customers no longer have                                  合約負債。客戶可在一至

                      Interest income is recognised using the                            利息收入以實際利率法確
                      effective interest method. The “effective                          認。「實際利率」為將於金
                      interest rate” is the rate that exactly                            融資產的預期使用壽命內
                      discounts estimated future cash receipts                           估計的未來現金收入準確
                      through the expected life of the financial                         折現至該金融資產的賬面
                      asset to the gross carrying amount of                              總值的比率。於計算利息
                      the financial asset. In calculating interest                       收入時，將實際利率應用
                      income, the effective interest rate is                             於資產的總賬面值（當資產
                      applied to the gross carrying amount of                            並無信貸減值時）。然而，
                      the asset (when the asset is not credit-                           就初始確認後出現信貸減
                      impaired). However, for financial assets                           值的金融資產而言，利息
                      that have become credit-impaired                                   收入以金融資產的攤銷成
                      subsequent to initial recognition, interest                        本應用實際利率計算。倘
                      income is calculated by applying the                               資產不再信貸減值，則恢
                      effective interest rate to the amortised                           復使用總額基準計算利息
                      cost of the financial asset. If the asset                          收入。
                      is no longer credit-impaired, then the
                      calculation of interest income reverts to
                      the gross basis.

    ESTIMATES
    Estimates and judgements are continually evaluated and are          估計及判斷會持續進行評估，並根據過往經
    based on historical experience and other factors, including         驗及其他因素而作出，包括對未來事件作出
    expectations of future events that are believed to be               認為在有關情況下屬合理的預期。
    reasonable under the circumstances.


    Key sources of estimation uncertainty in the preparation of         編製財務報表的估計不確定因素主要來源如
    the financial statements are as follows:                            下：


    (i)   Expected credit loss for trade and other                      (i)   貿易及其他應收款項的預期信貸虧
          receivables                                                         損
          The Group estimates impairment losses for trade                     本集團使用預期信貸虧損模型估計貿易
          and other receivables by using expected credit loss                 及其他應收款項的減值虧損。預期信貸
          models. Expected credit loss are estimated based on                 虧損乃基於本集團的過往信貸虧損經驗
          the Group’s historical credit loss experience, adjusted             進行估計，並就於報告日期債務人的特
          for factors that are specific to the debtors and an                 定因素及對當前及預計一般經濟狀況的
          assessment of both the current and forecast general                 評估進行調整。
          economic conditions at the end of reporting period.


          Where the expectation is different from the original                當預期與原定估計有差異時，該差異將
          estimate, such difference will impact the carrying                  影響該估計改變的期間內的貿易及其他
          amount of trade and other receivables and loss                      應收款項的賬面值及虧損撥備。本集團
          allowance in the periods in which such estimate is                  持續評估貿易及其他應收款項在其預期
          changed. The Group keeps assessing the ECLs of trade                年期內的預期信貸虧損。

   (e)   Capital management                                           (e)   資本管理
         The Group’s primary objectives when managing                       本集團管理資本的主要目標為保障本集
         capital are to safeguard the Group’s ability to                    團持續經營的能力，將產品及服務與風
         continue as a going concern, so that it can continue               險水平作相應計價，以合理的成本取得
         to provide returns for shareholders and benefits for               融資，向股東持續提供回報，為其他利
         other stakeholders, by pricing products and services               益相關者提供利益。
         commensurately with the level of risk and by securing
         access to finance at a reasonable cost.


         The Group actively and regularly reviews and manages               本集團積極定期審視及管理其資本結
         its capital structure to maintain a balance between                構，在較高借款水平可能帶來較高股東
         the higher shareholder returns that might be possible              回報及穩健資本狀況帶來的優勢與保障
         with higher levels of borrowings and the advantages                之間取得平衡，亦因應經濟狀況轉變調
         and security afforded by a sound capital position, and             整資本結構。
         makes adjustments to the capital structure in light of
         changes in economic conditions.


         Neither the Company nor any of its subsidiaries are                本公司或其任何附屬公司並無受外界施
         subject to externally imposed capital requirements.                加的資本要求所限。

                 financial loss to the Group. The Group’s credit risk is            團的信貸風險主要源自貿易及其他應收
                 primarily attributable to trade and other receivables              款項及合約資產。
                 and contract assets.

                 and cash equivalents, restricted deposits and other                存款及其他金融資產的信貸風險有限，
                 financial assets is limited because the counterparties             此乃由於交易方為知名銀行，本集團認
                 are well-known banks, which the Group considers                    為其信貸風險屬低。
                 representing low credit risk.


                 The Group’s exposure to credit risk is influenced                  本集團所面臨的信貸風險主要受各客戶
                 mainly by the individual characteristics of each                   的個別特徵的影響，而非來自客戶經營
                 customer rather than the industry or country in which              所在的行業或國家，因此，主要在本集
                 the customers operate and therefore significant                    團承受個別客戶的重大風險時產生高
                 concentrations of credit risk primarily arise when                 度集中的信貸風險。於2025年12月31
                 the Group has significant exposure to individual                   日，貿易應收款項及合約資產總額中
                 customers. At 31 December 2025, 41.5% (2024:                       的 41.5%（ 2024 年：18.0% ）及 57.5%

                 receivables and contract assets were due from the                  客戶及本集團五大客戶結欠。
                 Group’s largest customer and the Group’s five largest

                 Individual credit evaluations are performed on all                 對所有超過一定信貸金額的客戶進行個

                 of the year, including bank loans and lease liabilities,                         市場利率變動不會令本集團面臨利率風
                 and the change of market interest rate does not                                  險。總體而言，本集團面臨的利率風險
                 expose the Group to interest rate risk. Overall, the                             並不重大。
                 Group’s exposure to interest rate risk is not significant.

30 POSSIBLE IMPACT OF AMENDMENTS,                                     30 於年內已頒佈但尚未生效的修訂、
   NEW STANDARDS AND                                                     新準則及詮釋的潛在影響（續）
   INTERPRETATIONS ISSUED BUT NOT YET
   EFFECTIVE FOR THE YEAR (Continued)
   IFRS 18, Presentation and disclosure in financial                    國際財務報告準則第18號，財務報表呈報及
   statements                                                           披露


   IFRS 18 will replace IAS 1 Presentation of financial                 國際財務報告準則第18號將取代國際會計準
   statements and aims to improve the transparency and                  則第1號財務報表呈報，旨在提高實體財務
   comparability of information about an entity’s financial             報表相關信息的透明度及可比性。國際財務
   statements. IFRS 18 is effective for annual reporting periods        報告準則第18號於2027年1月1日或之後開始
   beginning on or after 1 January 2027 and is to be applied            的年度報告期間生效並將追溯應用。
   retrospectively.


   Among other changes, under IFRS 18, entities are required            除其他變動外，根據國際財務報告準則第18
   to classify all income and expenses into five categories             號，實體須於損益表中將所有收入及開支分
   in the statement of profit or loss, namely the operating,            類為五個類別，即經營、投資、融資、已終
   investing, financing, discontinued operations and income             止經營業務及所得稅類別。實體亦須在財務
   tax categories. Entities are also required to provide specific       報表的單一附註中提供有關管理層定義的績
   disclosures about management-defined performance                     效衡量標準的具體披露。
   measures in a single note in the financial statements.


   The Group does not plan to early adopt IFRS 18 and IFRS              本集團並不打算提早採用國際財務報告準則
   18 will impact the presentation of financial statements and          第18號。國際財務報告準則第18號將影響財
   is not expected to have significant impact on the financial          務報表呈報，且預期不會對本集團的財務表
   performance and positions of the Group.                              現及狀況造成重大影響。


   The Group is in the process of making an assessment of               本集團正在評估該等變動於初始應用期間的
   what the impact of these developments is expected to be in           預期影響。直至目前為止，本集團認為，除
   the period of initial application. So far it has concluded that,     上文所述國際財務報告準則第18號外，採納
   except for IFRS 18 as mentioned above, the adoption of               該等變動不大可能對本集團的綜合財務報表
   these developments is unlikely to have a significant impact          產生重大影響。
   on the Group’s consolidated financial statements.
